import Yoga, {type Node as YogaNode} from 'yoga-layout';
import measureText from './measure-text.js';
import {type Styles} from './styles.js';
import wrapText from './wrap-text.js';
import squashTextNodes from './squash-text-nodes.js';
import {type OutputTransformer} from './render-node-to-output.js';

type InkNode = {
	parentNode: DOMElement | undefined;
	yogaNode?: YogaNode;
	internal_static?: boolean;
	style: Styles;
};

type LayoutListener = () => void;

export type TextName = '#text';
export type ElementNames =
	| 'ink-root'
	| 'ink-box'
	| 'ink-text'
	| 'ink-virtual-text';

export type NodeNames = ElementNames | TextName;

// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/naming-convention
export type DOMElement = {
	nodeName: ElementNames;
	attributes: Record<string, DOMNodeAttribute>;
	childNodes: DOMNode[];
	internal_transform?: OutputTransformer;

	internal_accessibility?: {
		role?:
			| 'button'
			| 'checkbox'
			| 'combobox'
			| 'list'
			| 'listbox'
			| 'listitem'
			| 'menu'
			| 'menuitem'
			| 'option'
			| 'progressbar'
			| 'radio'
			| 'radiogroup'
			| 'tab'
			| 'tablist'
			| 'table'
			| 'textbox'
			| 'timer'
			| 'toolbar';
		state?: {
			busy?: boolean;
			checked?: boolean;
			disabled?: boolean;
			expanded?: boolean;
			multiline?: boolean;
			multiselectable?: boolean;
			readonly?: boolean;
			required?: boolean;
			selected?: boolean;
		};
	};

	// Internal properties
	isStaticDirty?: boolean;
	staticNode?: DOMElement;
	onComputeLayout?: () => void;
	onRender?: () => void;
	onImmediateRender?: () => void;
	internal_layoutListeners?: Set<LayoutListener>;
} & InkNode;

export type TextNode = {
	nodeName: TextName;
	nodeValue: string;
} & InkNode;

// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/naming-convention
export type DOMNode<T = {nodeName: NodeNames}> = T extends {
	nodeName: infer U;
}
	? U extends '#text'
		? TextNode
		: DOMElement
	: never;

// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/naming-convention
export type DOMNodeAttribute = boolean | string | number;

export const createNode = (nodeName: ElementNames): DOMElement => {
	const node: DOMElement = {
		nodeName,
		style: {},
		attributes: {},
		childNodes: [],
		parentNode: undefined,
		yogaNode: nodeName === 'ink-virtual-text' ? undefined : Yoga.Node.create(),
		// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/naming-convention
		internal_accessibility: {},
	};

	if (nodeName === 'ink-text') {
		node.yogaNode?.setMeasureFunc(measureTextNode.bind(null, node));
	}

	return node;
};

export const appendChildNode = (
	node: DOMElement,
	childNode: DOMElement,
): void => {
	if (childNode.parentNode) {
		removeChildNode(childNode.parentNode, childNode);
	}

	childNode.parentNode = node;
	node.childNodes.push(childNode);

	if (childNode.yogaNode) {
		node.yogaNode?.insertChild(
			childNode.yogaNode,
			node.yogaNode.getChildCount(),
		);
	}

	if (node.nodeName === 'ink-text' || node.nodeName === 'ink-virtual-text') {
		markNodeAsDirty(node);
	}
};

export const insertBeforeNode = (
	node: DOMElement,
	newChildNode: DOMNode,
	beforeChildNode: DOMNode,
): void => {
	if (newChildNode.parentNode) {
		removeChildNode(newChildNode.parentNode, newChildNode);
	}

	newChildNode.parentNode = node;

	const index = node.childNodes.indexOf(beforeChildNode);
	if (index >= 0) {
		node.childNodes.splice(index, 0, newChildNode);
		if (newChildNode.yogaNode) {
			node.yogaNode?.insertChild(newChildNode.yogaNode, index);
		}
	} else {
		node.childNodes.push(newChildNode);

		if (newChildNode.yogaNode) {
			node.yogaNode?.insertChild(
				newChildNode.yogaNode,
				node.yogaNode.getChildCount(),
			);
		}
	}

	if (node.nodeName === 'ink-text' || node.nodeName === 'ink-virtual-text') {
		markNodeAsDirty(node);
	}
};

export const removeChildNode = (
	node: DOMElement,
	removeNode: DOMNode,
): void => {
	if (removeNode.yogaNode) {
		removeNode.parentNode?.yogaNode?.removeChild(removeNode.yogaNode);
	}

	removeNode.parentNode = undefined;

	const index = node.childNodes.indexOf(removeNode);
	if (index >= 0) {
		node.childNodes.splice(index, 1);
	}

	if (node.nodeName === 'ink-text' || node.nodeName === 'ink-virtual-text') {
		markNodeAsDirty(node);
	}
};

export const setAttribute = (
	node: DOMElement,
	key: string,
	value: DOMNodeAttribute,
): void => {
	if (key === 'internal_accessibility') {
		node.internal_accessibility = value as DOMElement['internal_accessibility'];
		return;
	}

	node.attributes[key] = value;
};

export const setStyle = (node: DOMNode, style?: Styles): void => {
	// Rendering code assumes style is always an object.
	node.style = style ?? {};
};

export const createTextNode = (text: string): TextNode => {
	const node: TextNode = {
		nodeName: '#text',
		nodeValue: text,
		yogaNode: undefined,
		parentNode: undefined,
		style: {},
	};

	setTextNodeValue(node, text);

	return node;
};

const measureTextNode = function (
	node: DOMNode,
	width: number,
): {width: number; height: number} {
	const text =
		node.nodeName === '#text' ? node.nodeValue : squashTextNodes(node);

	const dimensions = measureText(text);

	// Text fits into container, no need to wrap
	if (dimensions.width <= width) {
		return dimensions;
	}

	// This is happening when <Box> is shrinking child nodes and Yoga asks
	// if we can fit this text node in a <1px space, so we just tell Yoga "no"
	if (dimensions.width >= 1 && width > 0 && width < 1) {
		return dimensions;
	}

	const textWrap = node.style?.textWrap ?? 'wrap';
	const wrappedText = wrapText(text, width, textWrap);

	return measureText(wrappedText);
};

const findClosestYogaNode = (node?: DOMNode): YogaNode | undefined => {
	if (!node?.parentNode) {
		return undefined;
	}

	return node.yogaNode ?? findClosestYogaNode(node.parentNode);
};

const markNodeAsDirty = (node?: DOMNode): void => {
	// Mark closest Yoga node as dirty to measure text dimensions again
	const yogaNode = findClosestYogaNode(node);
	yogaNode?.markDirty();
};

export const setTextNodeValue = (node: TextNode, text: string): void => {
	if (typeof text !== 'string') {
		text = String(text);
	}

	node.nodeValue = text;
	markNodeAsDirty(node);
};

export const addLayoutListener = (
	rootNode: DOMElement,
	listener: LayoutListener,
): (() => void) => {
	if (rootNode.nodeName !== 'ink-root') {
		return () => {};
	}

	rootNode.internal_layoutListeners ??= new Set();
	rootNode.internal_layoutListeners.add(listener);

	return () => {
		rootNode.internal_layoutListeners?.delete(listener);
	};
};

export const emitLayoutListeners = (rootNode: DOMElement): void => {
	if (rootNode.nodeName !== 'ink-root' || !rootNode.internal_layoutListeners) {
		return;
	}

	for (const listener of rootNode.internal_layoutListeners) {
		listener();
	}
};
=====RENDERER=====
import renderNodeToOutput, {
	renderNodeToScreenReaderOutput,
} from './render-node-to-output.js';
import Output from './output.js';
import {type DOMElement} from './dom.js';

type Result = {
	output: string;
	outputHeight: number;
	staticOutput: string;
};

const renderer = (node: DOMElement, isScreenReaderEnabled: boolean): Result => {
	if (node.yogaNode) {
		if (isScreenReaderEnabled) {
			const output = renderNodeToScreenReaderOutput(node, {
				skipStaticElements: true,
			});

			const outputHeight = output === '' ? 0 : output.split('\n').length;

			let staticOutput = '';

			if (node.staticNode) {
				staticOutput = renderNodeToScreenReaderOutput(node.staticNode, {
					skipStaticElements: false,
				});
			}

			return {
				output,
				outputHeight,
				staticOutput: staticOutput ? `${staticOutput}\n` : '',
			};
		}

		const output = new Output({
			width: node.yogaNode.getComputedWidth(),
			height: node.yogaNode.getComputedHeight(),
		});

		renderNodeToOutput(node, output, {
			skipStaticElements: true,
		});

		let staticOutput;

		if (node.staticNode?.yogaNode) {
			staticOutput = new Output({
				width: node.staticNode.yogaNode.getComputedWidth(),
				height: node.staticNode.yogaNode.getComputedHeight(),
			});

			renderNodeToOutput(node.staticNode, staticOutput, {
				skipStaticElements: false,
			});
		}

		const {output: generatedOutput, height: outputHeight} = output.get();

		return {
			output: generatedOutput,
			outputHeight,
			// Newline at the end is needed, because static output doesn't have one, so
			// interactive output will override last line of static output
			staticOutput: staticOutput ? `${staticOutput.get().output}\n` : '',
		};
	}

	return {
		output: '',
		outputHeight: 0,
		staticOutput: '',
	};
};

export default renderer;

=====RENDER-TO-STRING=====
     1	import type {ReactNode} from 'react';
     2	import Yoga from 'yoga-layout';
     3	import {LegacyRoot} from 'react-reconciler/constants.js';
     4	import reconciler from './reconciler.js';
     5	import renderer from './renderer.js';
     6	import {createNode, type DOMElement} from './dom.js';
     7	
     8	export type RenderToStringOptions = {
     9		/**
    10		Width of the virtual terminal in columns.
    11	
    12		@default 80
    13		*/
    14		columns?: number;
    15	};
    16	
    17	/**
    18	Render a React element to a string synchronously. Unlike `render()`, this function does not write to stdout, does not set up any terminal event listeners, and returns the rendered output as a string.
    19	
    20	Useful for generating documentation, writing output to files, testing, or any scenario where you need the rendered output as a string without starting a persistent terminal application.
    21	
    22	**Notes:**
    23	
    24	- Terminal-specific hooks (`useInput`, `useStdin`, `useStdout`, `useStderr`, `useApp`, `useFocus`, `useFocusManager`) return default no-op values since there is no terminal session. They will not throw, but they will not function as in a live terminal.
    25	- `useEffect` callbacks will execute during rendering (due to synchronous rendering mode), but state updates they trigger will not affect the returned output, which reflects the initial render.
    26	- `useLayoutEffect` callbacks fire synchronously during commit, so state updates they trigger **will** be reflected in the output.
    27	- The `<Static>` component is supported — its output is prepended to the dynamic output.
    28	- If a component throws during rendering, the error is propagated to the caller after cleanup.
    29	
    30	@example
    31	```
    32	import {renderToString, Text, Box} from 'ink';
    33	
    34	const output = renderToString(
    35		<Box padding={1}>
    36			<Text color="green">Hello World</Text>
    37		</Box>,
    38		{columns: 40}
    39	);
    40	
    41	console.log(output);
    42	```
    43	*/
    44	const renderToString = (
    45		node: ReactNode,
    46		options?: RenderToStringOptions,
    47	): string => {
    48		const columns = options?.columns ?? 80;
    49	
    50		// Create a standalone root node — no stdout, stdin, or terminal bindings
    51		const rootNode: DOMElement = createNode('ink-root');
    52	
    53		// Capture static output from intermediate renders.
    54		// The <Static> component uses useLayoutEffect to clear its children after
    55		// the first commit. The reconciler's resetAfterCommit calls onImmediateRender
    56		// when static content is dirty (and returns early, skipping the normal
    57		// onRender callback), giving us a chance to capture it before it's cleared
    58		// by the subsequent re-render.
    59		let capturedStaticOutput = '';
    60	
    61		rootNode.onComputeLayout = () => {
    62			rootNode.yogaNode!.setWidth(columns);
    63			rootNode.yogaNode!.calculateLayout(
    64				undefined,
    65				undefined,
    66				Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR,
    67			);
    68		};
    69	
    70		rootNode.onImmediateRender = () => {
    71			const {staticOutput} = renderer(rootNode, false);
    72			if (staticOutput && staticOutput !== '\n') {
    73				capturedStaticOutput += staticOutput;
    74			}
    75		};
    76	
    77		// Capture the first uncaught error so we can re-throw it after cleanup.
    78		// React's reconciler catches component errors internally and reports them
    79		// via onUncaughtError rather than letting them propagate. For a synchronous
    80		// utility like renderToString, callers expect errors to throw.
    81		let uncaughtError: unknown;
    82	
    83		// Create a reconciler container in legacy (synchronous) mode.
    84		// The four trailing callbacks are: onUncaughtError, onCaughtError,
    85		// onRecoverableError, and onHostTransitionComplete.
    86		// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unsafe-assignment
    87		const container = reconciler.createContainer(
    88			rootNode,
    89			LegacyRoot,
    90			null,
    91			false,
    92			null,
    93			'render-to-string',
    94			(error: unknown) => {
    95				uncaughtError ??= error;
    96			},
    97			() => {},
    98			() => {},
    99			() => {},
   100		);
   101	
   102		let teardownSucceeded = false;
   103	
   104		try {
   105			// Synchronously render the React tree into the container
   106			reconciler.updateContainerSync(node, container, null, () => {});
   107			reconciler.flushSyncWork();
   108	
   109			// Yoga layout has already been calculated by onComputeLayout during commit.
   110			// Render the DOM tree to a string — this captures the dynamic (non-static) output.
   111			const {output} = renderer(rootNode, false);
   112	
   113			// Tear down: unmount the tree so the reconciler cleans up child nodes
   114			// and runs effect cleanup functions. Child Yoga nodes are freed by the
   115			// reconciler's removeChildFromContainer → cleanupYogaNode → freeRecursive.
   116			reconciler.updateContainerSync(null, container, null, () => {});
   117			reconciler.flushSyncWork();
   118			teardownSucceeded = true;
   119	
   120			// Free the root yoga node itself (children already freed by reconciler)
   121			rootNode.yogaNode!.free();
   122	
   123			// Re-throw after full cleanup so callers see the original error.
   124			if (uncaughtError !== undefined) {
   125				throw uncaughtError instanceof Error
   126					? uncaughtError
   127					: // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-base-to-string
   128						new Error(String(uncaughtError));
   129			}
   130	
   131			// The renderer appends a trailing newline to static output for terminal
   132			// rendering (so dynamic output starts on a fresh line). Strip it here
   133			// so renderToString returns clean output.
   134			const normalizedStaticOutput = capturedStaticOutput.endsWith('\n')
   135				? capturedStaticOutput.slice(0, -1)
   136				: capturedStaticOutput;
   137	
   138			if (normalizedStaticOutput && output) {
   139				return normalizedStaticOutput + '\n' + output;
   140			}
   141	
   142			return normalizedStaticOutput || output;
   143		} finally {
   144			// Ensure native Yoga memory is freed even if rendering or teardown threw.
   145			// Yoga nodes are WASM-backed and not garbage collected.
   146			if (!teardownSucceeded && rootNode.yogaNode) {
   147				try {
   148					// If reconciler teardown failed, some child nodes may not have been
   149					// freed. Use freeRecursive to clean up the entire tree as best-effort.
   150					rootNode.yogaNode.freeRecursive();
   151				} catch {
   152					// Best-effort: node may already be partially freed

=====TESTS=====
import {act} from 'react';
import {render} from '../../src/index.js';
import createStdout from './create-stdout.js';

type RenderToStringOptions = {
	columns?: number;
	isScreenReaderEnabled?: boolean;
};

/**
Synchronous render to string (legacy mode).
*/
export const renderToString: (
	node: React.JSX.Element,
	options?: RenderToStringOptions,
) => string = (node, options) => {
	const stdout = createStdout(options?.columns ?? 100);

	render(node, {
		stdout,
		debug: true,
		isScreenReaderEnabled: options?.isScreenReaderEnabled,
	});

	const output = stdout.get();
	return output;
};

/**
Async render to string with concurrent mode support.

Uses `act()` to properly flush updates.
*/
export const renderToStringAsync: (
	node: React.JSX.Element,
	options?: RenderToStringOptions,
) => Promise<string> = async (node, options) => {
	const stdout = createStdout(options?.columns ?? 100);

	await act(async () => {
		render(node, {
			stdout,
			debug: true,
			isScreenReaderEnabled: options?.isScreenReaderEnabled,
			concurrent: true,
		});
	});

	const output = stdout.get();
	return output;
};
=====GAP TEST=====
import React from 'react';
import test from 'ava';
import {Box, Text} from '../src/index.js';
import {
	renderToString,
	renderToStringAsync,
} from './helpers/render-to-string.js';

test('gap', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box gap={1} width={3} flexWrap="wrap">
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A B\n\nC');
});

test('column gap', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box gap={1}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A B');
});

test('row gap', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box flexDirection="column" gap={1}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A\n\nB');
});

// Concurrent mode tests
test('gap - concurrent', async t => {
	const output = await renderToStringAsync(
		<Box gap={1} width={3} flexWrap="wrap">
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A B\n\nC');
});

test('column gap - concurrent', async t => {
	const output = await renderToStringAsync(
		<Box gap={1}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A B');
});

test('row gap - concurrent', async t => {
	const output = await renderToStringAsync(
		<Box flexDirection="column" gap={1}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);

	t.is(output, 'A\n\nB');
});

import Yoga from 'yoga-layout';

const root = Yoga.Node.create();
root.setWidth(20);
root.setHeight(10);

const child = Yoga.Node.create();
child.setMeasureFunc((width, widthMode, height, heightMode) => ({width: 4, height: 1}));
child.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE);
root.insertChild(child, 0);

root.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
console.log('child layout (absolute auto):', child.getComputedLayout());

child.setWidth(7);
child.setHeight(2);
child.setPosition(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT, 3);
child.setPosition(Yoga.EDGE_TOP, 1);
root.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
console.log('child layout (absolute explicit):', child.getComputedLayout());
EOF

import Yoga from 'yoga-layout';
const root = Yoga.Node.create();
root.setWidth(20);
root.setHeight(10);

const box = Yoga.Node.create();
box.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE);
const text = Yoga.Node.create();
text.setMeasureFunc((width) => ({width: Math.min(4, width), height: width >= 4 ? 1 : 2}));
box.insertChild(text, 0);
root.insertChild(box, 0);
root.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
console.log('box layout', box.getComputedLayout());
console.log('text layout', text.getComputedLayout());

// Now with flexDirection row and wrap
const root2 = Yoga.Node.create();
root2.setWidth(20);
root2.setHeight(10);
const box2 = Yoga.Node.create();
box2.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE);
const text2 = Yoga.Node.create();
text2.setMeasureFunc(() => ({width: 4, height: 1}));
box2.insertChild(text2, 0);
root2.insertChild(box2, 0);
root2.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
console.log('box2 layout', box2.getComputedLayout());
EOF
cd /app && node yoga-test4.mjs
OUTPUT:
box layout { left: 0, right: 0, top: 0, bottom: 0, width: 4, height: 1 }
text layout { left: 0, right: 0, top: 0, bottom: 0, width: 4, height: 1 }
box2 layout { left: 0, right: 0, top: 0, bottom: 0, width: 4, height: 1 }

cat > /app/src/grid.ts <<'EOF'
import Yoga, {type Node as YogaNode} from 'yoga-layout';
import {type DOMElement} from './dom.js';

type TrackSize =
	| {type: 'fixed'; value: number}
	| {type: 'fr'; value: number}
	| {type: 'auto'}
	| {
			type: 'minmax';
			min: number;
			max: {type: 'fixed'; value: number} | {type: 'fr'; value: number};
	  };

type LinePlacement = {
	start: number;
	end: number;
};

type AutoPlacement = {
	item: DOMElement;
	colStart: number;
	colEnd: number;
	rowStart: number;
	rowEnd: number;
};

type PlacedItem = {
	item: DOMElement;
	colStart: number;
	colEnd: number;
	rowStart: number;
	rowEnd: number;
	width: number;
	height: number;
	marginLeft: number;
	marginRight: number;
	marginTop: number;
	marginBottom: number;
};

const parseTrack = (token: string): TrackSize => {
	const value = token.trim();

	if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(value)) {
		return {type: 'fixed', value: Number(value)};
	}

	if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?fr$/.test(value)) {
		return {
			type: 'fr',
			value: Number(value.slice(0, -2)),
		};
	}

	if (value === 'auto') {
		return {type: 'auto'};
	}

	if (value.startsWith('minmax(') && value.endsWith(')')) {
		const inner = value.slice('minmax('.length, -1);
		const commaIndex = inner.indexOf(',');
		if (commaIndex === -1) {
			throw new Error(`Invalid grid track: ${token}`);
		}

		const min = Number(inner.slice(0, commaIndex).trim());
		const maxValue = inner.slice(commaIndex + 1).trim();

		if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(maxValue)) {
			return {
				type: 'minmax',
				min,
				max: {type: 'fixed', value: Number(maxValue)},
			};
		}

		if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?fr$/.test(maxValue)) {
			return {
				type: 'minmax',
				min,
				max: {type: 'fr', value: Number(maxValue.slice(0, -2))},
			};
		}

		throw new Error(`Invalid grid track: ${token}`);
	}

	throw new Error(`Invalid grid track: ${token}`);
};

const parseTrackList = (value?: string): TrackSize[] => {
	if (!value) {
		return [];
	}

	const tokens: string[] = [];
	let current = '';
	let depth = 0;

	for (const character of value.trim()) {
		if (character === '(') {
			depth++;
		}

		if (character === ')') {
			depth--;
		}

		if (character === ' ' && depth === 0) {
			if (current) {
				tokens.push(current);
				current = '';
			}
		} else {
			current += character;
		}
	}

	if (current) {
		tokens.push(current);
	}

	return tokens.map(parseTrack);
};

const parseLinePlacement = (
	value: number | string | undefined,
): LinePlacement | undefined => {
	if (value === undefined) {
		return undefined;
	}

	if (typeof value === 'number') {
		const start = Math.max(1, Math.floor(value));
		return {start, end: start + 1};
	}

	const parts = value.split('/').map(part => part.trim());

	if (parts.length === 1) {
		const start = Number(parts[0]);
		if (Number.isInteger(start) && start >= 1) {
			return {start, end: start + 1};
		}

		return undefined;
	}

	const start = Number(parts[0]);
	const end = Number(parts[1]);
	if (
		Number.isInteger(start) &&
		Number.isInteger(end) &&
		start >= 1 &&
		end > start
	) {
		return {start, end};
	}

	return undefined;
};

const getMargin = (
	node: YogaNode,
	edge: number,
): number => node.getComputedMargin(edge);

const getContentSize = (
	node: YogaNode,
	{isWidth}: {isWidth: boolean},
): number | undefined => {
	const value = isWidth ? node.getWidth() : node.getHeight();
	let total = 0;

	if (value.unit === Yoga.UNIT_POINT) {
		total = value.value;
	} else if (value.unit === Yoga.UNIT_PERCENT) {
		total = isWidth
			? node.getComputedWidth()
			: node.getComputedHeight();
	} else {
		return undefined;
	}

	const borderLeft = node.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
	const borderRight = node.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
	const borderTop = node.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const borderBottom = node.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);
	const paddingLeft = node.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
	const paddingRight = node.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
	const paddingTop = node.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const paddingBottom = node.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);

	if (isWidth) {
		return total - borderLeft - borderRight - paddingLeft - paddingRight;
	}

	return total - borderTop - borderBottom - paddingTop - paddingBottom;
};

const resolveAutoPlacement = (
	items: DOMElement[],
	columnCount: number,
	hasColumnTemplate: boolean,
): AutoPlacement[] => {
	const occupied = new Set<string>();
	const placements: AutoPlacement[] = [];
	let cursorRow = 1;

	const canPlace = (
		rowStart: number,
		colStart: number,
		rowEnd: number,
		colEnd: number,
	): boolean => {
		for (let row = rowStart; row < rowEnd; row++) {
			for (let column = colStart; column < colEnd; column++) {
				if (occupied.has(`${row}:${column}`)) {
					return false;
				}
			}
		}

		return true;
	};

	const occupy = (
		rowStart: number,
		colStart: number,
		rowEnd: number,
		colEnd: number,
	): void => {
		for (let row = rowStart; row < rowEnd; row++) {
			for (let column = colStart; column < colEnd; column++) {
				occupied.add(`${row}:${column}`);
			}
		}
	};

	for (const item of items) {
		const column = parseLinePlacement(item.style.gridColumn);
		const row = parseLinePlacement(item.style.gridRow);

		const colStart = column?.start;
		const colEnd = column?.end;
		const rowStart = row?.start;
		const rowEnd = row?.end;
		const columnSpan = colEnd ? colEnd - colStart! : 1;
		const rowSpan = rowEnd ? rowEnd - rowStart! : 1;

		let finalColStart: number;
		let finalColEnd: number;
		let finalRowStart: number;
		let finalRowEnd: number;

		if (colStart !== undefined && rowStart !== undefined) {
			finalColStart = colStart;
			finalColEnd = colEnd!;
			finalRowStart = rowStart;
			finalRowEnd = rowEnd!;
		} else if (colStart !== undefined) {
			let rowIndex = cursorRow;
			while (!canPlace(rowIndex, colStart, rowIndex + rowSpan, colEnd!)) {
				rowIndex++;
			}

			finalRowStart = rowIndex;
			finalRowEnd = rowIndex + rowSpan;
			finalColStart = colStart;
			finalColEnd = colEnd!;
		} else if (rowStart !== undefined) {
			let columnIndex = 1;
			while (
				!canPlace(rowStart, columnIndex, rowStart + rowSpan, columnIndex + columnSpan)
			) {
				columnIndex++;
			}

			finalRowStart = rowStart;
			finalRowEnd = rowStart + rowSpan;
			finalColStart = columnIndex;
			finalColEnd = columnIndex + columnSpan;
		} else {
			let found = false;
			let rowIndex = cursorRow;
			let columnIndex = 1;

			if (hasColumnTemplate) {
				while (!found) {
					while (columnIndex + columnSpan - 1 <= columnCount) {
						if (
							canPlace(
								rowIndex,
								columnIndex,
								rowIndex + rowSpan,
								columnIndex + columnSpan,
							)
						) {
							found = true;
							break;
						}

						columnIndex++;
					}

					if (found) {
						break;
					}

					rowIndex++;
					columnIndex = 1;
				}
			}

			finalRowStart = rowIndex;
			finalRowEnd = rowIndex + rowSpan;
			finalColStart = columnIndex;
			finalColEnd = columnIndex + columnSpan;
		}

		occupy(finalRowStart, finalColStart, finalRowEnd, finalColEnd);
		cursorRow = finalRowStart;
		placements.push({
			item,
			colStart: finalColStart,
			colEnd: finalColEnd,
			rowStart: finalRowStart,
			rowEnd: finalRowEnd,
		});
	}

	return placements;
};

const resolveTrackSizes = (
	tracks: TrackSize[],
	placements: Array<{start: number; end: number; size: number}>,
	gap: number,
	contentSize: number | undefined,
): number[] => {
	const sizes = new Array<number>(tracks.length).fill(0);
	const contentSizes = new Array<number>(tracks.length).fill(0);

	// Gather content contribution for each track
	for (const placement of placements) {
		const span = Math.max(1, placement.end - placement.start);
		const contribution = placement.size / span;

		for (let index = placement.start; index < placement.end; index++) {
			if (index >= 1 && index <= tracks.length) {
				contentSizes[index - 1] = Math.max(
					contentSizes[index - 1],
					contribution,
				);
			}
		}
	}

	const frTracks: Array<{index: number; fr: number}> = [];

	for (const [index, track] of tracks.entries()) {
		switch (track.type) {
			case 'fixed': {
				sizes[index] = track.value;
				break;
			}

			case 'auto': {
				sizes[index] = contentSizes[index];
				break;
			}

			case 'fr': {
				if (contentSize === undefined) {
					sizes[index] = contentSizes[index];
				} else {
					sizes[index] = 0;
					frTracks.push({index, fr: track.value});
				}

				break;
			}

			case 'minmax': {
				if (track.max.type === 'fixed') {
					sizes[index] = Math.min(
						track.max.value,
						Math.max(track.min, contentSizes[index]),
					);
				} else if (contentSize === undefined) {
					sizes[index] = Math.max(track.min, contentSizes[index]);
				} else {
					sizes[index] = track.min;
					frTracks.push({index, fr: track.max.value});
				}

				break;
			}
		}
	}

	if (contentSize !== undefined) {
		const totalGap = Math.max(0, tracks.length - 1) * gap;
		const used = sizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) + totalGap;
		const remaining = contentSize - used;

		if (remaining > 0) {
			const totalFr = frTracks.reduce((sum, {fr}) => sum + fr, 0);

			if (totalFr > 0) {
				for (const {index, fr} of frTracks) {
					sizes[index] += (remaining * fr) / totalFr;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	return sizes;
};

const getItemSize = (item: DOMElement): number | undefined => {
	const node = item.yogaNode;
	if (!node) {
		return undefined;
	}

	const width = node.getComputedWidth();
	const marginLeft = getMargin(node, Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
	const marginRight = getMargin(node, Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);

	return width + marginLeft + marginRight;
};

const getItemHeight = (item: DOMElement): number | undefined => {
	const node = item.yogaNode;
	if (!node) {
		return undefined;
	}

	const height = node.getComputedHeight();
	const marginTop = getMargin(node, Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const marginBottom = getMargin(node, Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);

	return height + marginTop + marginBottom;
};

const getTrackStart = (
	sizes: number[],
	start: number,
	gap: number,
): number => {
	let offset = 0;

	for (let index = 0; index < start - 1; index++) {
		offset += sizes[index] + gap;
	}

	return offset;
};

const getTrackSpanSize = (
	sizes: number[],
	start: number,
	end: number,
	gap: number,
): number => {
	let size = 0;

	for (let index = start - 1; index < end - 1; index++) {
		size += sizes[index];
	}

	size += Math.max(0, end - start - 1) * gap;
	return size;
};

const layoutGridContainer = (container: DOMElement): void => {
	const containerNode = container.yogaNode;
	if (!containerNode) {
		return;
	}

	const style = container.style;
	const items = container.childNodes.filter(
		(child): child is DOMElement =>
			child.nodeName !== '#text' &&
			Boolean(child.yogaNode) &&
			child.yogaNode!.getDisplay() !== Yoga.DISPLAY_NONE,
	);

	if (items.length === 0) {
		return;
	}

	const columnTemplates = parseTrackList(style.gridTemplateColumns);
	const rowTemplates = parseTrackList(style.gridTemplateRows);

	const explicitColumnCount = columnTemplates.length;
	const explicitRowCount = rowTemplates.length;

	const maximumExplicitColumnEnd = items.reduce((maximum, item) => {
		const placement = parseLinePlacement(item.style.gridColumn);
		return Math.max(maximum, placement?.end ?? 1);
	}, 1);

	const maximumExplicitRowEnd = items.reduce((maximum, item) => {
		const placement = parseLinePlacement(item.style.gridRow);
		return Math.max(maximum, placement?.end ?? 1);
	}, 1);

	const columnCount = Math.max(
		explicitColumnCount,
		maximumExplicitColumnEnd,
		1,
	);

	const autoPlacements = resolveAutoPlacement(
		items,
		columnCount,
		explicitColumnCount > 0,
	);

	const effectiveColumnCount = Math.max(
		columnCount,
		...autoPlacements.map(placement => placement.colEnd),
	);

	const effectiveRowCount = Math.max(
		explicitRowCount,
		...autoPlacements.map(placement => placement.rowEnd),
		1,
	);

	const columnTrackSizes: TrackSize[] = [
		...columnTemplates,
		...new Array<TrackSize>(
			Math.max(0, effectiveColumnCount - columnTemplates.length),
		).fill({type: 'auto'}),
	];

	const rowTrackSizes: TrackSize[] = [
		...rowTemplates,
		...new Array<TrackSize>(
			Math.max(0, effectiveRowCount - rowTemplates.length),
		).fill({type: 'auto'}),
	];

	const columnGap = style.columnGap ?? style.gap ?? 0;
	const rowGap = style.rowGap ?? style.gap ?? 0;

	const containerWidth = getContentSize(containerNode, {isWidth: true});
	const columnPlacements = autoPlacements.map(placement => ({
		start: placement.colStart,
		end: placement.colEnd,
		size: getItemSize(placement.item) ?? 0,
	}));

	const columnSizes = resolveTrackSizes(
		columnTrackSizes,
		columnPlacements,
		columnGap,
		containerWidth,
	);

	const columnTotal =
		columnSizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) +
		Math.max(0, columnSizes.length - 1) * columnGap;

	const borderLeft = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
	const borderRight = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
	const borderTop = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const borderBottom = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);
	const paddingLeft = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
	const paddingRight = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
	const paddingTop = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const paddingBottom = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);

	if (containerWidth === undefined) {
		containerNode.setWidth(
			columnTotal + borderLeft + borderRight + paddingLeft + paddingRight,
		);
	}

	const placedItems: PlacedItem[] = [];

	for (const placement of autoPlacements) {
		const itemNode = placement.item.yogaNode!;
		const columnStart = getTrackStart(columnSizes, placement.colStart, columnGap);
		const columnSpanSize = getTrackSpanSize(
			columnSizes,
			placement.colStart,
			placement.colEnd,
			columnGap,
		);

		const marginLeft = getMargin(itemNode, Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
		const marginRight = getMargin(itemNode, Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
		const marginTop = getMargin(itemNode, Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
		const marginBottom = getMargin(itemNode, Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);

		const widthValue = itemNode.getWidth();
		const hasExplicitWidth = widthValue.unit !== Yoga.UNIT_AUTO;

		const itemWidth = hasExplicitWidth
			? Math.max(0, widthValue.value - marginLeft - marginRight)
			: Math.max(0, columnSpanSize - marginLeft - marginRight);

		itemNode.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE);
		itemNode.setPosition(
			Yoga.EDGE_LEFT,
			borderLeft + paddingLeft + columnStart - marginLeft,
		);
		itemNode.setWidth(itemWidth);

		placedItems.push({
			item: placement.item,
			colStart: placement.colStart,
			colEnd: placement.colEnd,
			rowStart: placement.rowStart,
			rowEnd: placement.rowEnd,
			width: itemWidth,
			height: 0,
			marginLeft,
			marginRight,
			marginTop,
			marginBottom,
		});
	}
};

const isGridContainer = (node: DOMElement): boolean =>
	node.style.display === 'grid';

const restoreGridState = (node: DOMElement): void => {
	const state = node.internal_grid;
	const yogaNode = node.yogaNode;

	if (!state || !yogaNode) {
		return;
	}

	if (state.width.unit === Yoga.UNIT_POINT) {
		yogaNode.setWidth(state.width.value);
	} else if (state.width.unit === Yoga.UNIT_PERCENT) {
		yogaNode.setWidthPercent(state.width.value);
	} else {
		yogaNode.setWidthAuto();
	}

	if (state.height.unit === Yoga.UNIT_POINT) {
		yogaNode.setHeight(state.height.value);
	} else if (state.height.unit === Yoga.UNIT_PERCENT) {
		yogaNode.setHeightPercent(state.height.value);
	} else {
		yogaNode.setHeightAuto();
	}
};

const storeGridState = (node: DOMElement): void => {
	const yogaNode = node.yogaNode;
	if (!yogaNode || node.internal_grid) {
		return;
	}

	node.internal_grid = {
		width: yogaNode.getWidth(),
		height: yogaNode.getHeight(),
		positionType: yogaNode.getPositionType(),
	};
};

const resetGridLayout = (rootNode: DOMElement): boolean => {
	let foundGrid = false;

	const visit = (node: DOMElement): void => {
		const isGrid = isGridContainer(node);

		if (node.internal_grid) {
			restoreGridState(node);

			if (node.parentNode && !isGridContainer(node.parentNode)) {
				node.yogaNode?.setPositionType(node.internal_grid.positionType);
				delete node.internal_grid;
			}
		}

		if (isGrid) {
			foundGrid = true;

			if (node.yogaNode) {
				for (const child of node.childNodes) {
					if (
						child.nodeName === '#text' ||
						!child.yogaNode ||
						child.yogaNode.getDisplay() === Yoga.DISPLAY_NONE
					) {
						continue;
					}

					storeGridState(child);
					child.yogaNode.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE);
				}
			}
		}

		for (const child of node.childNodes) {
			if (child.nodeName !== '#text') {
				visit(child);
			}
		}
	};

	visit(rootNode);
	return foundGrid;
};

const applyGridLayout = (rootNode: DOMElement): boolean => {
	let foundGrid = false;

	const visit = (node: DOMElement): void => {
		if (isGridContainer(node)) {
			foundGrid = true;
			layoutGridContainer(node);
		}

		for (const child of node.childNodes) {
			if (child.nodeName !== '#text') {
				visit(child);
			}
		}
	};

	visit(rootNode);
	return foundGrid;
};

/**
Calculate layout for the whole tree, including CSS grid support.

Yoga doesn't support grid layout natively, so grid containers are laid out
manually. Before calculating layout, grid children are reset to their natural
size and measured. Then grid tracks are resolved and children are positioned
using absolute positioning. Finally layout is calculated again so Yoga applies
the manually computed positions and sizes.
*/
export const calculateLayout = (rootNode: DOMElement): void => {
	const hasGrid = resetGridLayout(rootNode);

	rootNode.yogaNode?.calculateLayout(
		undefined,
		undefined,
		Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR,
	);

	if (hasGrid) {
		const hasActiveGrid = applyGridLayout(rootNode);

		if (hasActiveGrid) {
			// Second pass to lay out rows after columns have been resolved
			rootNode.yogaNode?.calculateLayout(
				undefined,
				undefined,
				Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR,
			);
		}
	}
};

import Yoga, {type Node as YogaNode} from 'yoga-layout';
import {type DOMElement} from './dom.js';

type TrackSize =
	| {type: 'fixed'; value: number}
	| {type: 'fr'; value: number}
	| {type: 'auto'}
	| {
			type: 'minmax';
			min: number;
			max: {type: 'fixed'; value: number} | {type: 'fr'; value: number};
	  };

type LinePlacement = {
	start: number;
	end: number;
};

type AutoPlacement = {
	item: DOMElement;
	colStart: number;
	colEnd: number;
	rowStart: number;
	rowEnd: number;
};

type PlacedItem = {
	item: DOMElement;
	colStart: number;
	colEnd: number;
	rowStart: number;
	rowEnd: number;
	marginLeft: number;
	marginRight: number;
	marginTop: number;
	marginBottom: number;
};

type GridLayout = {
	placedItems: PlacedItem[];
	columnSizes: number[];
	columnGap: number;
	rowGap: number;
};

const parseTrack = (token: string): TrackSize => {
	const value = token.trim();

	if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(value)) {
		return {type: 'fixed', value: Number(value)};
	}

	if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?fr$/.test(value)) {
		return {type: 'fr', value: Number(value.slice(0, -2))};
	}

	if (value === 'auto') {
		return {type: 'auto'};
	}

	if (value.startsWith('minmax(') && value.endsWith(')')) {
		const inner = value.slice('minmax('.length, -1);
		const commaIndex = inner.indexOf(',');
		if (commaIndex === -1) {
			throw new Error(`Invalid grid track: ${token}`);
		}

		const min = Number(inner.slice(0, commaIndex).trim());
		const maxValue = inner.slice(commaIndex + 1).trim();

		if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(maxValue)) {
			return {
				type: 'minmax',
				min,
				max: {type: 'fixed', value: Number(maxValue)},
			};
		}

		if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?fr$/.test(maxValue)) {
			return {
				type: 'minmax',
				min,
				max: {type: 'fr', value: Number(maxValue.slice(0, -2))},
			};
		}

		throw new Error(`Invalid grid track: ${token}`);
	}

	throw new Error(`Invalid grid track: ${token}`);
};

const parseTrackList = (value?: string): TrackSize[] => {
	if (!value) {
		return [];
	}

	const tokens: string[] = [];
	let current = '';
	let depth = 0;

	for (const character of value.trim()) {
		if (character === '(') {
			depth++;
		}

		if (character === ')') {
			depth--;
		}

		if (character === ' ' && depth === 0) {
			if (current) {
				tokens.push(current);
				current = '';
			}
		} else {
			current += character;
		}
	}

	if (current) {
		tokens.push(current);
	}

	return tokens.map(parseTrack);
};

const parseLinePlacement = (
	value: number | string | undefined,
): LinePlacement | undefined => {
	if (value === undefined) {
		return undefined;
	}

	if (typeof value === 'number') {
		const start = Math.max(1, Math.floor(value));
		return {start, end: start + 1};
	}

	const parts = value.split('/').map(part => part.trim());

	if (parts.length === 1) {
		const start = Number(parts[0]);
		if (Number.isInteger(start) && start >= 1) {
			return {start, end: start + 1};
		}

		return undefined;
	}

	const start = Number(parts[0]);
	const end = Number(parts[1]);
	if (
		Number.isInteger(start) &&
		Number.isInteger(end) &&
		start >= 1 &&
		end > start
	) {
		return {start, end};
	}

	return undefined;
};

const getMargin = (node: YogaNode, edge: number): number =>
	node.getComputedMargin(edge);

const getContentSize = (
	node: YogaNode,
	isWidth: boolean,
): number | undefined => {
	const value = isWidth ? node.getWidth() : node.getHeight();
	let total = 0;

	if (value.unit === Yoga.UNIT_POINT) {
		total = value.value;
	} else if (value.unit === Yoga.UNIT_PERCENT) {
		total = isWidth ? node.getComputedWidth() : node.getComputedHeight();
	} else {
		return undefined;
	}

	const borderLeft = node.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
	const borderRight = node.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
	const borderTop = node.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const borderBottom = node.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);
	const paddingLeft = node.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
	const paddingRight = node.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
	const paddingTop = node.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const paddingBottom = node.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);

	if (isWidth) {
		return total - borderLeft - borderRight - paddingLeft - paddingRight;
	}

	return total - borderTop - borderBottom - paddingTop - paddingBottom;
};

const resolveAutoPlacement = (
	items: DOMElement[],
	columnCount: number,
	hasColumnTemplate: boolean,
): AutoPlacement[] => {
	const occupied = new Set<string>();
	const placements: AutoPlacement[] = [];
	let cursorRow = 1;

	const canPlace = (
		rowStart: number,
		colStart: number,
		rowEnd: number,
		colEnd: number,
	): boolean => {
		for (let row = rowStart; row < rowEnd; row++) {
			for (let column = colStart; column < colEnd; column++) {
				if (occupied.has(`${row}:${column}`)) {
					return false;
				}
			}
		}

		return true;
	};

	const occupy = (
		rowStart: number,
		colStart: number,
		rowEnd: number,
		colEnd: number,
	): void => {
		for (let row = rowStart; row < rowEnd; row++) {
			for (let column = colStart; column < colEnd; column++) {
				occupied.add(`${row}:${column}`);
			}
		}
	};

	for (const item of items) {
		const column = parseLinePlacement(item.style.gridColumn);
		const row = parseLinePlacement(item.style.gridRow);

		const colStart = column?.start;
		const colEnd = column?.end;
		const rowStart = row?.start;
		const rowEnd = row?.end;
		const columnSpan = colEnd ? colEnd - colStart! : 1;
		const rowSpan = rowEnd ? rowEnd - rowStart! : 1;

		let finalColStart: number;
		let finalColEnd: number;
		let finalRowStart: number;
		let finalRowEnd: number;

		if (colStart !== undefined && rowStart !== undefined) {
			finalColStart = colStart;
			finalColEnd = colEnd!;
			finalRowStart = rowStart;
			finalRowEnd = rowEnd!;
		} else if (colStart !== undefined) {
			let rowIndex = cursorRow;
			while (!canPlace(rowIndex, colStart, rowIndex + rowSpan, colEnd!)) {
				rowIndex++;
			}

			finalRowStart = rowIndex;
			finalRowEnd = rowIndex + rowSpan;
			finalColStart = colStart;
			finalColEnd = colEnd!;
		} else if (rowStart !== undefined) {
			let columnIndex = 1;
			while (
				!canPlace(
					rowStart,
					columnIndex,
					rowStart + rowSpan,
					columnIndex + columnSpan,
				)
			) {
				columnIndex++;
			}

			finalRowStart = rowStart;
			finalRowEnd = rowStart + rowSpan;
			finalColStart = columnIndex;
			finalColEnd = columnIndex + columnSpan;
		} else {
			let found = false;
			let rowIndex = cursorRow;
			let columnIndex = 1;

			if (hasColumnTemplate) {
				while (!found) {
					while (columnIndex + columnSpan - 1 <= columnCount) {
						if (
							canPlace(
								rowIndex,
								columnIndex,
								rowIndex + rowSpan,
								columnIndex + columnSpan,
							)
						) {
							found = true;
							break;
						}

						columnIndex++;
					}

					if (found) {
						break;
					}

					rowIndex++;
					columnIndex = 1;
				}
			}

			finalRowStart = rowIndex;
			finalRowEnd = rowIndex + rowSpan;
			finalColStart = columnIndex;
			finalColEnd = columnIndex + columnSpan;
		}

		occupy(finalRowStart, finalColStart, finalRowEnd, finalColEnd);
		cursorRow = finalRowStart;
		placements.push({
			item,
			colStart: finalColStart,
			colEnd: finalColEnd,
			rowStart: finalRowStart,
			rowEnd: finalRowEnd,
		});
	}

	return placements;
};

const resolveTrackSizes = (
	tracks: TrackSize[],
	placements: Array<{start: number; end: number; size: number}>,
	gap: number,
	contentSize: number | undefined,
): number[] => {
	const sizes = new Array<number>(tracks.length).fill(0);
	const contentSizes = new Array<number>(tracks.length).fill(0);

	for (const placement of placements) {
		const span = Math.max(1, placement.end - placement.start);
		const contribution = placement.size / span;

		for (let index = placement.start; index < placement.end; index++) {
			if (index >= 1 && index <= tracks.length) {
				contentSizes[index - 1] = Math.max(
					contentSizes[index - 1],
					contribution,
				);
			}
		}
	}

	const frTracks: Array<{index: number; fr: number}> = [];

	for (const [index, track] of tracks.entries()) {
		switch (track.type) {
			case 'fixed': {
				sizes[index] = track.value;
				break;
			}

			case 'auto': {
				sizes[index] = contentSizes[index];
				break;
			}

			case 'fr': {
				if (contentSize === undefined) {
					sizes[index] = contentSizes[index];
				} else {
					sizes[index] = 0;
					frTracks.push({index, fr: track.value});
				}

				break;
			}

			case 'minmax': {
				if (track.max.type === 'fixed') {
					sizes[index] = Math.min(
						track.max.value,
						Math.max(track.min, contentSizes[index]),
					);
				} else if (contentSize === undefined) {
					sizes[index] = Math.max(track.min, contentSizes[index]);
				} else {
					sizes[index] = track.min;
					frTracks.push({index, fr: track.max.value});
				}

				break;
			}
		}
	}

	if (contentSize !== undefined) {
		const totalGap = Math.max(0, tracks.length - 1) * gap;
		const used = sizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) + totalGap;
		const remaining = contentSize - used;

		if (remaining > 0) {
			const totalFr = frTracks.reduce((sum, {fr}) => sum + fr, 0);

			if (totalFr > 0) {
				for (const {index, fr} of frTracks) {
					sizes[index] += (remaining * fr) / totalFr;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	return sizes;
};

const getItemOuterSize = (
	item: DOMElement,
	isWidth: boolean,
): number | undefined => {
	const node = item.yogaNode;
	if (!node) {
		return undefined;
	}

	const size = isWidth
		? node.getComputedWidth()
		: node.getComputedHeight();
	const marginLeft = isWidth ? getMargin(node, Yoga.EDGE_LEFT) : 0;
	const marginRight = isWidth ? getMargin(node, Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT) : 0;
	const marginTop = isWidth ? 0 : getMargin(node, Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const marginBottom = isWidth ? 0 : getMargin(node, Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);

	return size + marginLeft + marginRight + marginTop + marginBottom;
};

const getTrackStart = (
	sizes: number[],
	start: number,
	gap: number,
): number => {
	let offset = 0;

	for (let index = 0; index < start - 1; index++) {
		offset += sizes[index] + gap;
	}

	return offset;
};

const getTrackSpanSize = (
	sizes: number[],
	start: number,
	end: number,
	gap: number,
): number => {
	let size = 0;

	for (let index = start - 1; index < end - 1; index++) {
		size += sizes[index];
	}

	size += Math.max(0, end - start - 1) * gap;
	return size;
};

const getGridItems = (container: DOMElement): DOMElement[] =>
	container.childNodes.filter(
		(child): child is DOMElement =>
			child.nodeName !== '#text' &&
			Boolean(child.yogaNode) &&
			child.yogaNode!.getDisplay() !== Yoga.DISPLAY_NONE,
	);

const resolvePlacements = (container: DOMElement): AutoPlacement[] => {
	const items = getGridItems(container);
	const columnTemplates = parseTrackList(container.style.gridTemplateColumns);

	const maximumExplicitColumnEnd = items.reduce((maximum, item) => {
		const placement = parseLinePlacement(item.style.gridColumn);
		return Math.max(maximum, placement?.end ?? 1);
	}, 1);

	const columnCount = Math.max(columnTemplates.length, maximumExplicitColumnEnd, 1);

	return resolveAutoPlacement(items, columnCount, columnTemplates.length > 0);
};

const applyGridColumns = (rootNode: DOMElement): boolean => {
	let foundGrid = false;

	const visit = (node: DOMElement): void => {
		if (isGridContainer(node)) {
			foundGrid = true;
			layoutGridColumns(node);
		}

		for (const child of node.childNodes) {
			if (child.nodeName !== '#text') {
				visit(child);
			}
		}
	};

	visit(rootNode);
	return foundGrid;
};

const layoutGridColumns = (container: DOMElement): void => {
	const containerNode = container.yogaNode;
	if (!containerNode || getGridItems(container).length === 0) {
		return;
	}

	const style = container.style;
	const columnTemplates = parseTrackList(style.gridTemplateColumns);
	const rowTemplates = parseTrackList(style.gridTemplateRows);
	const autoPlacements = resolvePlacements(container);

	const effectiveColumnCount = Math.max(
		columnTemplates.length,
		...autoPlacements.map(placement => placement.colEnd),
		1,
	);

	const effectiveRowCount = Math.max(
		rowTemplates.length,
		...autoPlacements.map(placement => placement.rowEnd),
		1,
	);

	const columnTrackSizes: TrackSize[] = [
		...columnTemplates,
		...new Array<TrackSize>(
			Math.max(0, effectiveColumnCount - columnTemplates.length),
		).fill({type: 'auto'}),
	];

	const rowTrackSizes: TrackSize[] = [
		...rowTemplates,
		...new Array<TrackSize>(
			Math.max(0, effectiveRowCount - rowTemplates.length),
		).fill({type: 'auto'}),
	];

	const columnGap = style.columnGap ?? style.gap ?? 0;
	const rowGap = style.rowGap ?? style.gap ?? 0;

	const containerWidth = getContentSize(containerNode, true);
	const columnPlacements = autoPlacements.map(placement => ({
		start: placement.colStart,
		end: placement.colEnd,
		size: getItemOuterSize(placement.item, true) ?? 0,
	}));

	const columnSizes = resolveTrackSizes(
		columnTrackSizes,
		columnPlacements,
		columnGap,
		containerWidth,
	);

	const columnTotal =
		columnSizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) +
		Math.max(0, columnSizes.length - 1) * columnGap;

	const borderLeft = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
	const borderRight = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
	const borderTop = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const borderBottom = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);
	const paddingLeft = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
	const paddingRight = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
	const paddingTop = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const paddingBottom = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);

	if (containerWidth === undefined) {
		containerNode.setWidth(
			columnTotal + borderLeft + borderRight + paddingLeft + paddingRight,
		);
	}

	const placedItems: PlacedItem[] = [];

	for (const placement of autoPlacements) {
		const itemNode = placement.item.yogaNode!;
		const columnStart = getTrackStart(columnSizes, placement.colStart, columnGap);
		const columnSpanSize = getTrackSpanSize(
			columnSizes,
			placement.colStart,
			placement.colEnd,
			columnGap,
		);

		const marginLeft = getMargin(itemNode, Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
		const marginRight = getMargin(itemNode, Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
		const marginTop = getMargin(itemNode, Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
		const marginBottom = getMargin(itemNode, Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);

		const widthValue = itemNode.getWidth();
		const hasExplicitWidth =
			widthValue.unit === Yoga.UNIT_POINT ||
			widthValue.unit === Yoga.UNIT_PERCENT;

		let itemWidth: number;

		if (hasExplicitWidth && widthValue.unit === Yoga.UNIT_POINT) {
			itemWidth = Math.max(0, widthValue.value - marginLeft - marginRight);
		} else {
			itemWidth = Math.max(0, columnSpanSize - marginLeft - marginRight);
		}

		itemNode.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE);
		itemNode.setPosition(
			Yoga.EDGE_LEFT,
			borderLeft + paddingLeft + columnStart - marginLeft,
		);
		itemNode.setWidth(itemWidth);

		placedItems.push({
			item: placement.item,
			colStart: placement.colStart,
			colEnd: placement.colEnd,
			rowStart: placement.rowStart,
			rowEnd: placement.rowEnd,
			marginLeft,
			marginRight,
			marginTop,
			marginBottom,
		});
	}

	container.internal_gridLayout = {
		placedItems,
		columnSizes,
		columnGap,
		rowGap,
	};
};

const applyGridRows = (rootNode: DOMElement): boolean => {
	let foundGrid = false;

	const visit = (node: DOMElement): void => {
		if (isGridContainer(node)) {
			foundGrid = true;
			layoutGridRows(node);
		}

		for (const child of node.childNodes) {
			if (child.nodeName !== '#text') {
				visit(child);
			}
		}
	};

	visit(rootNode);
	return foundGrid;
};

const layoutGridRows = (container: DOMElement): void => {
	const containerNode = container.yogaNode;
	const gridLayout = container.internal_gridLayout;

	if (!containerNode || !gridLayout || gridLayout.placedItems.length === 0) {
		return;
	}

	const rowTemplates = parseTrackList(container.style.gridTemplateRows);
	const {placedItems, columnSizes, columnGap, rowGap} = gridLayout;

	const effectiveRowCount = Math.max(
		rowTemplates.length,
		...placedItems.map(item => item.rowEnd),
		1,
	);

	const rowTrackSizes: TrackSize[] = [
		...rowTemplates,
		...new Array<TrackSize>(
			Math.max(0, effectiveRowCount - rowTemplates.length),
		).fill({type: 'auto'}),
	];

	const containerHeight = getContentSize(containerNode, false);
	const rowPlacements = placedItems.map(item => ({
		start: item.rowStart,
		end: item.rowEnd,
		size: getItemOuterSize(item.item, false) ?? 0,
	}));

	const rowSizes = resolveTrackSizes(
		rowTrackSizes,
		rowPlacements,
		rowGap,
		containerHeight,
	);

	const rowTotal =
		rowSizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) +
		Math.max(0, rowSizes.length - 1) * rowGap;

	const borderLeft = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
	const borderRight = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
	const borderTop = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const borderBottom = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);
	const paddingLeft = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
	const paddingRight = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
	const paddingTop = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
	const paddingBottom = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);

	if (containerHeight === undefined) {
		containerNode.setHeight(
			rowTotal + borderTop + borderBottom + paddingTop + paddingBottom,
		);
	}

	for (const item of placedItems) {
		const itemNode = item.item.yogaNode!;
		const rowStart = getTrackStart(rowSizes, item.rowStart, rowGap);
		const rowSpanSize = getTrackSpanSize(
			rowSizes,
			item.rowStart,
			item.rowEnd,
			rowGap,
		);

		const heightValue = itemNode.getHeight();
		const hasExplicitHeight =
			heightValue.unit === Yoga.UNIT_POINT ||
			heightValue.unit === Yoga.UNIT_PERCENT;

		let itemHeight: number;

		if (hasExplicitHeight && heightValue.unit === Yoga.UNIT_POINT) {
			itemHeight = Math.max(
				0,
				heightValue.value - item.marginTop - item.marginBottom,
			);
		} else {
			itemHeight = Math.max(
				0,
				rowSpanSize - item.marginTop - item.marginBottom,
			);
		}

		itemNode.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE);
		itemNode.setPosition(
			Yoga.EDGE_TOP,
			borderTop + paddingTop + rowStart - item.marginTop,
		);
		itemNode.setHeight(itemHeight);
	}
};

const isGridContainer = (node: DOMElement): boolean =>
	node.style.display === 'grid';

const restoreGridState = (node: DOMElement): void => {
	const state = node.internal_grid;
	const yogaNode = node.yogaNode;

	if (!state || !yogaNode) {
		return;
	}

	if (state.width.unit === Yoga.UNIT_POINT) {
		yogaNode.setWidth(state.width.value);
	} else if (state.width.unit === Yoga.UNIT_PERCENT) {
		yogaNode.setWidthPercent(state.width.value);
	} else {
		yogaNode.setWidthAuto();
	}

	if (state.height.unit === Yoga.UNIT_POINT) {
		yogaNode.setHeight(state.height.value);
	} else if (state.height.unit === Yoga.UNIT_PERCENT) {
		yogaNode.setHeightPercent(state.height.value);
	} else {
		yogaNode.setHeightAuto();
	}
};

const storeGridState = (node: DOMElement): void => {
	const yogaNode = node.yogaNode;
	if (!yogaNode || node.internal_grid) {
		return;
	}

	node.internal_grid = {
		width: yogaNode.getWidth(),
		height: yogaNode.getHeight(),
		positionType: yogaNode.getPositionType(),
	};
};

const resetGridLayout = (rootNode: DOMElement): boolean => {
	let foundGrid = false;

	const visit = (node: DOMElement): void => {
		const isGrid = isGridContainer(node);

		if (node.internal_grid) {
			restoreGridState(node);

			if (node.parentNode && !isGridContainer(node.parentNode)) {
				node.yogaNode?.setPositionType(node.internal_grid.positionType);
				delete node.internal_grid;
			}
		}

		if (isGrid) {
			foundGrid = true;

			if (node.yogaNode) {
				storeGridState(node);

				for (const child of node.childNodes) {
					if (
						child.nodeName === '#text' ||
						!child.yogaNode ||
						child.yogaNode.getDisplay() === Yoga.DISPLAY_NONE
					) {
						continue;
					}

					storeGridState(child);
					child.yogaNode.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE);
				}
			}
		}

		for (const child of node.childNodes) {
			if (child.nodeName !== '#text') {
				visit(child);
			}
		}
	};

	visit(rootNode);
	return foundGrid;
};

/**
Calculate layout for the whole tree, including CSS grid support.

Yoga doesn't support grid layout natively, so grid containers are laid out
manually. The layout process runs in three passes:

1. Grid children are reset to their natural size and measured by Yoga.
2. Column tracks are resolved and children are positioned horizontally.
3. Row tracks are resolved using the heights produced by the second pass and
   children are positioned vertically.
*/
export const calculateLayout = (rootNode: DOMElement): void => {
	const hasGrid = resetGridLayout(rootNode);

	rootNode.yogaNode?.calculateLayout(
		undefined,
		undefined,
		Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR,
	);

	if (hasGrid) {
		const hasActiveGrid = applyGridColumns(rootNode);

		if (hasActiveGrid) {
			rootNode.yogaNode?.calculateLayout(
				undefined,
				undefined,
				Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR,
			);

			applyGridRows(rootNode);

			rootNode.yogaNode?.calculateLayout(
				undefined,
				undefined,
				Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR,
			);
		}
	}
};

   666	const layoutGridRows = (container: DOMElement): void => {
   667		const containerNode = container.yogaNode;

   669

   671			return;
   672		}
   673	
   674		const rowTemplates = parseTrackList(container.style.gridTemplateRows);
   675		const {placedItems, columnSizes, columnGap, rowGap} = gridLayout;
   676

   678			rowTemplates.length,
   679			...placedItems.map(item => item.rowEnd),
   680			1,
   681		);

   684			...rowTemplates,
   685			...new Array<TrackSize>(
   686				Math.max(0, effectiveRowCount - rowTemplates.length),
   687			).fill({type: 'auto'}),
   688		];
   689

   691		const rowPlacements = placedItems.map(item => ({
   692			start: item.rowStart,
   693			end: item.rowEnd,
   694			size: getItemOuterSize(item.item, false) ?? 0,
   695		}));

   699			rowPlacements,
   700			rowGap,
   701			containerHeight,

   706			Math.max(0, rowSizes.length - 1) * rowGap;

   588				itemWidth = Math.max(0, widthValue.value - marginLeft - marginRight);
   589			} else {
   590				itemWidth = Math.max(0, columnSpanSize - marginLeft - marginRight);
   591			}

   596				borderLeft + paddingLeft + columnStart - marginLeft,

   605				rowEnd: placement.rowEnd,

   608				marginTop,
   609				marginBottom,
   610			});
   611		}

   621	const applyGridRows = (rootNode: DOMElement): boolean => {
   622		let foundGrid = false;
   623	
   624		const visit = (node: DOMElement): void => {
   625			if (isGridContainer(node)) {
   626				foundGrid = true;
   627				layoutGridRows(node);
   628			}
   629	
   630			for (const child of node.childNodes) {
   631				if (child.nodeName !== '#text') {
   632					visit(child);
   633				}
   634			}
   635		};
   636	
   637		visit(rootNode);
   638		return foundGrid;
   639	};

path = 'src/grid.ts'
with open(path) as f:
    lines = f.readlines()

# line numbers are 1-based
def replace_line(lineno, old, new):
    idx = lineno - 1
    assert old in lines[idx], f"line {lineno} mismatch: {lines[idx]!r}"
    lines[idx] = lines[idx].replace(old, new)

# 365: sizes[index] = contentSizes[index];
replace_line(365, "sizes[index] = contentSizes[index];", "sizes[index] = contentSizes[index]!;")
# 387: Math.max(track.min, contentSizes[index])
replace_line(387, "Math.max(track.min, contentSizes[index])", "Math.max(track.min, contentSizes[index]!)")
# 445: offset += sizes[index] + gap;
replace_line(445, "offset += sizes[index] + gap;", "offset += sizes[index]! + gap;")
# 460: size += sizes[index];
replace_line(460, "size += sizes[index];", "size += sizes[index]!;")

# Fix layoutGridColumns border variables: lines 552-555
replace_line(552, "const borderTop = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);", "const borderLeft = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);")
replace_line(553, "const borderBottom = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);", "const borderRight = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);")
replace_line(554, "const paddingTop = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);", "const paddingLeft = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);")
replace_line(555, "const paddingBottom = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);", "const paddingRight = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);")

with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.writelines(lines)
print("done")

# Find the section and modify. Let's view relevant part first to do precise edit.

for i, line in enumerate(lines):
    if "cursorRow = hasColumnTemplate ? finalRowStart : finalRowEnd;" in line:
        print("found at line", i+1)
        # We need to make cursorRow update conditional on row being auto.
        # Replace with:

        )

import React, {useState} from 'react';
import {Box, Text, render} from './src/index.js';
import createStdout from './test/helpers/create-stdout.js';

function App() {
	const [count, setCount] = useState(0);
	const [grid, setGrid] = useState(true);
	return (
		<>
			<Box display={grid ? 'grid' : 'flex'} gridTemplateColumns="5 5" width={10}>
				<Text>A</Text>
				<Text>{String(count)}</Text>
			</Box>
			<Box>
				<Text onClick={() => setCount(c => c + 1)}>inc</Text>
				<Text onClick={() => setGrid(g => !g)}>toggle</Text>
			</Box>
		</>
	);
}

const stdout = createStdout(20);
render(<App />, {stdout, debug: true});
console.log('initial:', JSON.stringify(stdout.get()));

COMMAND:
cd /app && cat test/helpers/test-renderer.ts | head -100
OUTPUT:
import {act} from 'react';
import {render, type Instance} from '../../src/index.js';
import createStdout from './create-stdout.js';

type TestRenderOptions = {
	columns?: number;
	isScreenReaderEnabled?: boolean;
};

export type TestInstance = Instance & {
	stdout: ReturnType<typeof createStdout>;
	getOutput: () => string;
	rerenderAsync: (node: React.ReactNode) => Promise<void>;
};

/**
Render helper that supports concurrent mode with `act()` wrapping.

Uses `act()` to properly flush updates in concurrent mode.
*/
export async function renderAsync(
	node: React.ReactNode,
	options: TestRenderOptions = {},
): Promise<TestInstance> {
	const stdout = createStdout(options.columns ?? 100);

	let instance!: Instance;

	await act(async () => {
		instance = render(node, {
			stdout,
			debug: true,
			concurrent: true,
			isScreenReaderEnabled: options.isScreenReaderEnabled,
		});
	});

	return {
		...instance,
		stdout,
		getOutput: () => stdout.get(),
		async rerenderAsync(newNode: React.ReactNode) {
			await act(async () => {
				instance.rerender(newNode);
			});
		},
	};
}

/**
Synchronous render for legacy mode tests (backward compatible).
*/
export function renderSync(
	node: React.ReactNode,
	options: TestRenderOptions = {},
): TestInstance {
	const stdout = createStdout(options.columns ?? 100);

	const instance = render(node, {
		stdout,
		debug: true,
		concurrent: false,
		isScreenReaderEnabled: options.isScreenReaderEnabled,
	});

	return {
		...instance,
		stdout,
		getOutput: () => stdout.get(),
		async rerenderAsync(newNode: React.ReactNode) {
			instance.rerender(newNode);
		},
	};
}

/**
Wrapper to make existing sync code work with concurrent mode.

Use this to gradually migrate tests.
*/
export async function withAct<T>(fn: () => T | Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
	let result!: T;
	await act(async () => {
		result = await fn();
	});
	return result;
}

/**
Wait for pending suspense boundaries to resolve.
*/
export async function waitForSuspense(ms = 0): Promise<void> {
	await act(async () => {
		await new Promise<void>(resolve => {
			setTimeout(resolve, ms);
		});
	});
}

cat > /app/grid-dynamic2.tsx <<'EOF'
import React from 'react';
import {Box, Text} from './src/index.js';
import {renderAsync} from './test/helpers/test-renderer.js';

async function main() {
	const instance = await renderAsync(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" width={10}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 10},
	);
	console.log('grid:', JSON.stringify(instance.getOutput()));

	// Add more items -> new auto row
	await instance.rerenderAsync(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" width={10}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
			<Text>D</Text>
		</Box>,
	);
	console.log('grid 4 items:', JSON.stringify(instance.getOutput()));

	// Switch to flex
	await instance.rerenderAsync(
		<Box display="flex" width={10}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
			<Text>D</Text>
		</Box>,
	);
	console.log('flex:', JSON.stringify(instance.getOutput()));

	// Back to grid with fr
	await instance.rerenderAsync(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="1fr 2fr" width={12}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);
	console.log('grid fr:', JSON.stringify(instance.getOutput()));

	await instance.unmount();
}

main().catch(error => {
	console.error(error);
	process.exit(1);
});
EOF

const tests: Array<[string, React.JSX.Element, number]> = [
	['margin item', <Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" width={12}><Box marginRight={1}><Text>A</Text></Box><Text>B</Text></Box>, 12],
	['nested grid', <Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="10 10" width={20}><Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="1fr 1fr"><Text>AB</Text><Text>CD</Text></Box><Text>B</Text></Box>, 20],
	['minmax different mins', <Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="minmax(10, 1fr) minmax(20, 1fr)" width={40}><Text>A</Text><Text>B</Text></Box>, 40],
	['span with gap', <Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5 5" gap={1} width={17}><Box gridColumn="1 / 3"><Text>AB</Text></Box><Text>C</Text></Box>, 17],
];

for (const [name, node, columns] of tests) {
	try {
		const output = await renderToStringAsync(node, {columns});
		console.log(`--- ${name} (concurrent) ---`);
		console.log(JSON.stringify(output));
	} catch (error) {
		console.log(`--- ${name} ERROR ---`);
		console.error(error);
	}
}
EOF

path = 'src/grid.ts'
with open(path) as f:
    src = f.read()

# 1. tokens.map(parseTrack) -> arrow
src = src.replace("\treturn tokens.map(parseTrack);", "\treturn tokens.map(token => parseTrack(token));")

# 2. no-negated-condition: replace `!== undefined` checks in resolveAutoPlacement with truthy checks
# We'll replace within resolveAutoPlacement only. Do targeted replacements.
src = src.replace("if (colStart !== undefined && rowStart !== undefined) {", "if (colStart && rowStart) {")
src = src.replace("} else if (colStart !== undefined) {", "} else if (colStart) {")
src = src.replace("} else if (rowStart !== undefined) {", "} else if (rowStart) {")

# 3. blank line before placements.push
src = src.replace("\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tplacements.push({", "\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tplacements.push({", 1)

# 4. new Array for sizes/contentSizes
src = src.replace("const sizes = new Array<number>(tracks.length).fill(0);", "const sizes = Array.from({length: tracks.length}, () => 0);")
src = src.replace("const contentSizes = new Array<number>(tracks.length).fill(0);", "const contentSizes = Array.from({length: tracks.length}, () => 0);")

# 5. new Array TrackSize in layoutGridColumns and layoutGridRows
src = src.replace(
    """\tconst columnTrackSizes: TrackSize[] = [

)

# 6. reduce -> loop in resolvePlacements
old = """\tconst maximumExplicitColumnEnd = items.reduce((maximum, item) => {

new = """\tlet maximumExplicitColumnEnd = 1;

\t\t);
\t}"""
assert old in src

# 7. prefer-destructuring: const style = container.style
src = src.replace("\tconst style = container.style;\n\tconst columnTemplates = parseTrackList(style.gridTemplateColumns);", "\tconst {style} = container;\n\tconst columnTemplates = parseTrackList(style.gridTemplateColumns);")

# 8. prefer-ternary itemWidth
old = """\t\t\tlet itemWidth: number;

cat > /app/grid-empty-dynamic.tsx <<'EOF'
import React from 'react';
import {Box, Text} from './src/index.js';
import {renderAsync} from './test/helpers/test-renderer.js';

async function main() {
	const instance = await renderAsync(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" width={10}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 10},
	);
	console.log('initial:', JSON.stringify(instance.getOutput()));

	// Remove all children
	await instance.rerenderAsync(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" width={10} />,
	);
	console.log('empty:', JSON.stringify(instance.getOutput()));

	// Add children back
	await instance.rerenderAsync(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" width={10}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
		</Box>,
	);
	console.log('again:', JSON.stringify(instance.getOutput()));

	await instance.unmount();
}

main().catch(error => {
	console.error(error);
	process.exit(1);
});
EOF
cd /app && npx tsx grid-empty-dynamic.tsx 2>&1 | head -30

STEP 120 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cat > /app/test/grid.tsx <<'EOF'
import React from 'react';
import test from 'ava';
import {Box, Text} from '../src/index.js';
import {
	renderToString,
	renderToStringAsync,
} from './helpers/render-to-string.js';

test('display grid places children into columns', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="10 10" width={20}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
			<Text>D</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 20},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A         B\nC         D');
});

test('grid gap applies to tracks', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" gap={1} width={11}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 11},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A     B');
});

test('grid column and row gap apply to tracks', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box
			display="grid"
			gridTemplateColumns="3 3"
			columnGap={1}
			rowGap={1}
			width={7}
		>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
			<Text>D</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 7},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A   B\n\nC   D');
});

test('fr columns distribute remaining space', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="1fr 2fr" width={12}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 12},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A   B');
});

test('minmax with fr max distributes remaining space after minimums', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box
			display="grid"
			gridTemplateColumns="minmax(10, 1fr) minmax(20, 1fr)"
			width={40}
		>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 40},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A              B');
});

test('rows are created automatically as needed', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" width={10}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
			<Text>D</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 10},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A    B\nC    D');
});

test('grid without column template uses a single column', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box display="grid" width={10}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 10},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A\nB');
});

test('children can be explicitly placed with gridColumn and gridRow', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box
			display="grid"
			gridTemplateColumns="5 5"
			gridTemplateRows="5 5"
			width={10}
			height={10}
		>
			<Box gridColumn="2" gridRow="2">
				<Text>D</Text>
			</Box>
			<Box>
				<Text>A</Text>
			</Box>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 10},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A\n\n\n\n\n     D\n\n\n\n');
});

test('gridColumn and gridRow accept start / end string', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5 5" width={15}>
			<Box gridColumn="1 / 3">
				<Text>AB</Text>
			</Box>
			<Text>C</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 15},
	);

	t.is(output, 'AB        C');
});

test('Text children can be explicitly placed', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" width={10}>
			<Text gridColumn="2">B</Text>
			<Text gridColumn="1">A</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 10},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A    B');
});

test('grid - concurrent', async t => {
	const output = await renderToStringAsync(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" width={10}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 10},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A    B\nC');
});

     9		| {type: 'fr'; value: number}
    10		| {type: 'auto'}

    42	const parseTrack = (token: string): TrackSize => {
    43		const value = token.trim();
    44	
    45		if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(value)) {
    46			return {type: 'fixed', value: Number(value)};
    47		}
    48	
    49		if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?fr$/.test(value)) {
    50			return {type: 'fr', value: Number(value.slice(0, -2))};
    51		}
    52	
    53		if (value === 'auto') {
    54			return {type: 'auto'};
    55		}
    56	
    57		if (value.startsWith('minmax(') && value.endsWith(')')) {
    58			const inner = value.slice('minmax('.length, -1);
    59			const commaIndex = inner.indexOf(',');
    60			if (commaIndex === -1) {
    61				throw new Error(`Invalid grid track: ${token}`);
    62			}
    63	
    64			const min = Number(inner.slice(0, commaIndex).trim());
    65			const maxValue = inner.slice(commaIndex + 1).trim();
    66	
    67			if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(maxValue)) {
    68				return {
    69					type: 'minmax',
    70					min,
    71					max: {type: 'fixed', value: Number(maxValue)},
    72				};
    73			}
    74	
    75			if (/^\d+(\.\d+)?fr$/.test(maxValue)) {
    76				return {
    77					type: 'minmax',
    78					min,
    79					max: {type: 'fr', value: Number(maxValue.slice(0, -2))},
    80				};
    81			}
    82	
    83			throw new Error(`Invalid grid track: ${token}`);
    84		}
    85	
    86		throw new Error(`Invalid grid track: ${token}`);
    87	};
    88	
    89	const parseTrackList = (value?: string): TrackSize[] => {
    90		if (!value) {
    91			return [];
    92		}
    93	
    94		const tokens: string[] = [];
    95		let current = '';
    96		let depth = 0;
    97	
    98		for (const character of value.trim()) {
    99			if (character === '(') {
   100				depth++;
   101			}
   102	
   103			if (character === ')') {
   104				depth--;
   105			}
   106	
   107			if (character === ' ' && depth === 0) {
   108				if (current) {
   109					tokens.push(current);
   110					current = '';
   111				}
   112			} else {
   113				current += character;
   114			}
   115		}
   116	
   117		if (current) {
   118			tokens.push(current);
   119		}
   120	
   121		return tokens.map(token => parseTrack(token));
   122	};
   123	
   124	const parseLinePlacement = (
   125		value: number | string | undefined,
   126	): LinePlacement | undefined => {
   127		if (value === undefined) {
   128			return undefined;
   129		}
   130	
   131		if (typeof value === 'number') {
   132			const start = Math.max(1, Math.floor(value));
   133			return {start, end: start + 1};
   134		}
   135	
   136		const parts = value.split('/').map(part => part.trim());
   137	
   138		if (parts.length === 1) {
   139			const start = Number(parts[0]);
   140			if (Number.isInteger(start) && start >= 1) {
   141				return {start, end: start + 1};
   142			}
   143	
   144			return undefined;
   145		}
   146	
   147		const start = Number(parts[0]);
   148		const end = Number(parts[1]);
   149		if (
   150			Number.isInteger(start) &&
   151			Number.isInteger(end) &&
   152			start >= 1 &&
   153			end > start
   154		) {
   155			return {start, end};
   156		}
   157	
   158		return undefined;
   159	};

   161	const getMargin = (node: YogaNode, edge: number): number =>
   162		node.getComputedMargin(edge);
   163	
   164	const getContentSize = (
   165		node: YogaNode,
   166		isWidth: boolean,
   167	): number | undefined => {
   168		const value = isWidth ? node.getWidth() : node.getHeight();
   169		let total = 0;
   170	
   171		if (Number(value.unit) === unitPoint) {
   172			total = value.value;
   173		} else if (Number(value.unit) === unitPercent) {
   174			total = isWidth ? node.getComputedWidth() : node.getComputedHeight();
   175		} else {
   176			return undefined;
   177		}
   178	
   179		const borderLeft = node.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
   180		const borderRight = node.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
   181		const borderTop = node.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
   182		const borderBottom = node.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);
   183		const paddingLeft = node.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
   184		const paddingRight = node.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
   185		const paddingTop = node.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
   186		const paddingBottom = node.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);
   187	
   188		if (isWidth) {
   189			return total - borderLeft - borderRight - paddingLeft - paddingRight;
   190		}
   191	
   192		return total - borderTop - borderBottom - paddingTop - paddingBottom;
   193	};

   209		): boolean => {
   210			for (let row = rowStart; row < rowEnd; row++) {
   211				for (let column = colStart; column < colEnd; column++) {
   212					if (occupied.has(`${row}:${column}`)) {
   213						return false;
   214					}
   215				}
   216			}
   217	
   218			return true;
   219		};

   222			rowStart: number,
   223			colStart: number,
   224			rowEnd: number,

   227			for (let row = rowStart; row < rowEnd; row++) {

   229					occupied.add(`${row}:${column}`);

   238			const colStart = column?.start;
   239			const colEnd = column?.end;

   241			const rowEnd = row?.end;
   242			const columnSpan = colEnd ? colEnd - colStart! : 1;
   243			const rowSpan = rowEnd ? rowEnd - rowStart! : 1;

   245			let finalColStart: number;

   247			let finalRowStart: number;
