   337	const resolveTrackSizes = (
   338		tracks: TrackSize[],
   339		placements: Array<{start: number; end: number; size: number}>,
   340		gap: number,
   341		contentSize: number | undefined,
   342	): number[] => {
   343		const sizes = Array.from({length: tracks.length}, () => 0);
   344		const contentSizes = Array.from({length: tracks.length}, () => 0);
   345	
   346		for (const placement of placements) {
   347			const span = Math.max(1, placement.end - placement.start);
   348			const contribution = placement.size / span;
   349	
   350			for (let index = placement.start; index < placement.end; index++) {
   351				if (index >= 1 && index <= tracks.length) {
   352					contentSizes[index - 1] = Math.max(
   353						contentSizes[index - 1]!,
   354						contribution,
   355					);
   356				}
   357			}
   358		}
   359	
   360		const frTracks: Array<{index: number; fr: number}> = [];
   361	
   362		for (const [index, track] of tracks.entries()) {
   363			switch (track.type) {
   364				case 'fixed': {
   365					sizes[index] = track.value;
   366					break;
   367				}
   368	
   369				case 'auto': {
   370					sizes[index] = contentSizes[index]!;
   371					break;
   372				}
   373	
   374				case 'fr': {
   375					if (contentSize === undefined) {
   376						sizes[index] = contentSizes[index]!;
   377					} else {
   378						sizes[index] = 0;
   379						frTracks.push({index, fr: track.value});
   380					}
   381	
   382					break;
   383				}
   384	
   385				case 'minmax': {
   386					if (track.max.type === 'fixed') {
   387						sizes[index] = Math.min(
   388							track.max.value,
   389							Math.max(track.min, contentSizes[index]!),
   390						);
   391					} else if (contentSize === undefined) {
   392						sizes[index] = Math.max(track.min, contentSizes[index]!);
   393					} else {
   394						sizes[index] = track.min;
   395						frTracks.push({index, fr: track.max.value});
   396					}
   397	
   398					break;
   399				}
   400			}
   401		}
   402	
   403		if (contentSize !== undefined) {
   404			const totalGap = Math.max(0, tracks.length - 1) * gap;
   405			const used = sizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) + totalGap;
   406			const remaining = contentSize - used;
   407	
   408			if (remaining > 0) {
   409				const totalFr = frTracks.reduce((sum, {fr}) => sum + fr, 0);
   410	
   411				if (totalFr > 0) {
   412					for (const {index, fr} of frTracks) {
   413						sizes[index]! += (remaining * fr) / totalFr;
   414					}
   415				}
   416			}
   417		}
   418	
   419		return sizes;
   420	};
   421	
   422	const getItemOuterSize = (
   423		item: DOMElement,
   424		isWidth: boolean,
   425	): number | undefined => {
   426		const node = item.yogaNode;
   427		if (!node) {
   428			return undefined;
   429		}
   430	
   431		const size = isWidth ? node.getComputedWidth() : node.getComputedHeight();
   432		const marginLeft = isWidth ? getMargin(node, Yoga.EDGE_LEFT) : 0;
   433		const marginRight = isWidth ? getMargin(node, Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT) : 0;
   434		const marginTop = isWidth ? 0 : getMargin(node, Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
   435		const marginBottom = isWidth ? 0 : getMargin(node, Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);
   436	
   437		return size + marginLeft + marginRight + marginTop + marginBottom;
   438	};
   439	
   440	const getTrackStart = (sizes: number[], start: number, gap: number): number => {
   441		let offset = 0;
   442	
   443		for (let index = 0; index < start - 1; index++) {
   444			offset += sizes[index]! + gap;
   445		}
   446	
   447		return offset;
   448	};
   449	
   450	const getTrackSpanSize = (
   451		sizes: number[],
   452		start: number,
   453		end: number,
   454		gap: number,
   455	): number => {
   456		let size = 0;
   457	
   458		for (let index = start - 1; index < end - 1; index++) {
   459			size += sizes[index]!;
   460		}

OUTPUT:
   460		}
   461	
   462		size += Math.max(0, end - start - 1) * gap;
   463		return size;
   464	};
   465	
   466	const getGridItems = (container: DOMElement): DOMElement[] =>
   467		container.childNodes.filter(
   468			(child): child is DOMElement =>
   469				child.nodeName !== '#text' &&
   470				Boolean(child.yogaNode) &&
   471				child.yogaNode!.getDisplay() !== Yoga.DISPLAY_NONE,
   472		);
   473	
   474	const resolvePlacements = (container: DOMElement): AutoPlacement[] => {
   475		const items = getGridItems(container);
   476		const columnTemplates = parseTrackList(container.style.gridTemplateColumns);
   477	
   478		let maximumExplicitColumnEnd = 1;
   479	
   480		for (const item of items) {
   481			const placement = parseLinePlacement(item.style.gridColumn);
   482			maximumExplicitColumnEnd = Math.max(
   483				maximumExplicitColumnEnd,
   484				placement ? placement.end - 1 : 1,
   485			);
   486		}
   487	
   488		const columnCount = Math.max(
   489			columnTemplates.length,
   490			maximumExplicitColumnEnd,
   491			1,
   492		);
   493	
   494		return resolveAutoPlacement(items, columnCount, columnTemplates.length > 0);
   495	};
   496	
   497	const applyGridColumns = (rootNode: DOMElement): boolean => {
   498		let foundGrid = false;
   499	
   500		const visit = (node: DOMElement): void => {
   501			if (isGridContainer(node)) {
   502				foundGrid = true;
   503				layoutGridColumns(node);
   504			}
   505	
   506			for (const child of node.childNodes) {
   507				if (child.nodeName !== '#text') {
   508					visit(child);
   509				}
   510			}
   511		};
   512	
   513		visit(rootNode);
   514		return foundGrid;
   515	};
   516	
   517	const layoutGridColumns = (container: DOMElement): void => {
   518		const containerNode = container.yogaNode;
   519		if (!containerNode || getGridItems(container).length === 0) {
   520			delete container.internal_gridLayout;
   521			return;
   522		}
   523	
   524		const {style} = container;
   525		const columnTemplates = parseTrackList(style.gridTemplateColumns);
   526		const autoPlacements = resolvePlacements(container);
   527	
   528		const effectiveColumnCount = Math.max(
   529			columnTemplates.length,
   530			...autoPlacements.map(placement => placement.colEnd - 1),
   531			1,
   532		);
   533		const columnTrackSizes: TrackSize[] = [
   534			...columnTemplates,
   535			...Array.from(
   536				{length: Math.max(0, effectiveColumnCount - columnTemplates.length)},
   537				() => ({type: 'auto' as const}),
   538			),
   539		];
   540	
   541		const columnGap = style.columnGap ?? style.gap ?? 0;
   542		const rowGap = style.rowGap ?? style.gap ?? 0;
   543	
   544		const containerWidth = getContentSize(containerNode, true);
   545		const columnPlacements = autoPlacements.map(placement => ({
   546			start: placement.colStart,
   547			end: placement.colEnd,
   548			size: getItemOuterSize(placement.item, true) ?? 0,
   549		}));
   550	
   551		const columnSizes = resolveTrackSizes(
   552			columnTrackSizes,
   553			columnPlacements,
   554			columnGap,
   555			containerWidth,
   556		);
   557	
   558		const columnTotal =
   559			columnSizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) +
   560			Math.max(0, columnSizes.length - 1) * columnGap;
   561	
   562		const borderLeft = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
   563		const borderRight = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
   564		const paddingLeft = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
   565		const paddingRight = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
   566	
   567		if (containerWidth === undefined) {
   568			containerNode.setWidth(
   569				columnTotal + borderLeft + borderRight + paddingLeft + paddingRight,
   570			);
   571		}
   572	
   573		const placedItems: PlacedItem[] = [];
   574	
   575		for (const placement of autoPlacements) {
   576			const itemNode = placement.item.yogaNode!;
   577			const columnStart = getTrackStart(
   578				columnSizes,
   579				placement.colStart,
   580				columnGap,
   581			);
   582			const columnSpanSize = getTrackSpanSize(
   583				columnSizes,
   584				placement.colStart,
   585				placement.colEnd,
   586				columnGap,
   587			);
   588	
   589			const marginLeft = getMargin(itemNode, Yoga.EDGE_LEFT);
   590			const marginRight = getMargin(itemNode, Yoga.EDGE_RIGHT);
   591			const marginTop = getMargin(itemNode, Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
   592			const marginBottom = getMargin(itemNode, Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);
   593	
   594			const widthValue = itemNode.getWidth();
   595			const hasExplicitWidth =
   596				Number(widthValue.unit) === unitPoint ||
   597				Number(widthValue.unit) === unitPercent;
   598	
   599			const itemWidth =
   600				hasExplicitWidth && Number(widthValue.unit) === unitPoint
   601					? Math.max(0, widthValue.value - marginLeft - marginRight)
   602					: Math.max(0, columnSpanSize - marginLeft - marginRight);
   603	
   604			itemNode.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE);
   605			itemNode.setPosition(
   606				Yoga.EDGE_LEFT,
   607				borderLeft + paddingLeft + columnStart - marginLeft,
   608			);
   609			itemNode.setWidth(itemWidth);
   610	
   611			placedItems.push({
   612				item: placement.item,
   613				colStart: placement.colStart,
   614				colEnd: placement.colEnd,
   615				rowStart: placement.rowStart,
   616				rowEnd: placement.rowEnd,
   617				marginLeft,
   618				marginRight,
   619				marginTop,
   620				marginBottom,

   620				marginBottom,
   621			});
   622		}
   623	
   624		container.internal_gridLayout = {
   625			placedItems,
   626			columnSizes,
   627			columnGap,
   628			rowGap,
   629		};
   630	};
   631	
   632	const applyGridRows = (rootNode: DOMElement): boolean => {
   633		let foundGrid = false;
   634	
   635		const visit = (node: DOMElement): void => {
   636			if (isGridContainer(node)) {
   637				foundGrid = true;
   638				layoutGridRows(node);
   639			}
   640	
   641			for (const child of node.childNodes) {
   642				if (child.nodeName !== '#text') {
   643					visit(child);
   644				}
   645			}
   646		};
   647	
   648		visit(rootNode);
   649		return foundGrid;
   650	};
   651	
   652	const layoutGridRows = (container: DOMElement): void => {
   653		const containerNode = container.yogaNode;
   654		const gridLayout = container.internal_gridLayout;
   655	
   656		if (!containerNode || !gridLayout || gridLayout.placedItems.length === 0) {
   657			return;
   658		}
   659	
   660		const rowTemplates = parseTrackList(container.style.gridTemplateRows);
   661		const {placedItems, rowGap} = gridLayout;
   662	
   663		const effectiveRowCount = Math.max(
   664			rowTemplates.length,
   665			...placedItems.map(item => item.rowEnd - 1),
   666			1,
   667		);
   668	
   669		const rowTrackSizes: TrackSize[] = [
   670			...rowTemplates,
   671			...Array.from(
   672				{length: Math.max(0, effectiveRowCount - rowTemplates.length)},
   673				() => ({type: 'auto' as const}),
   674			),
   675		];
   676	
   677		const containerHeight = getContentSize(containerNode, false);
   678		const rowPlacements = placedItems.map(item => ({
   679			start: item.rowStart,
   680			end: item.rowEnd,
   681			size: getItemOuterSize(item.item, false) ?? 0,
   682		}));
   683	
   684		const rowSizes = resolveTrackSizes(
   685			rowTrackSizes,
   686			rowPlacements,
   687			rowGap,
   688			containerHeight,
   689		);
   690	
   691		const rowTotal =
   692			rowSizes.reduce((sum, size) => sum + size, 0) +
   693			Math.max(0, rowSizes.length - 1) * rowGap;
   694	
   695		const borderTop = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
   696		const borderBottom = containerNode.getComputedBorder(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);
   697		const paddingTop = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_TOP);
   698		const paddingBottom = containerNode.getComputedPadding(Yoga.EDGE_BOTTOM);
   699	
   700		if (containerHeight === undefined) {
   701			containerNode.setHeight(
   702				rowTotal + borderTop + borderBottom + paddingTop + paddingBottom,
   703			);
   704		}
   705	
   706		for (const item of placedItems) {
   707			const itemNode = item.item.yogaNode!;
   708			const rowStart = getTrackStart(rowSizes, item.rowStart, rowGap);
   709			const rowSpanSize = getTrackSpanSize(
   710				rowSizes,
   711				item.rowStart,
   712				item.rowEnd,
   713				rowGap,
   714			);
   715	
   716			const heightValue = itemNode.getHeight();
   717			const hasExplicitHeight =
   718				Number(heightValue.unit) === unitPoint ||
   719				Number(heightValue.unit) === unitPercent;
   720	
   721			let itemHeight: number;
   722	
   723			if (hasExplicitHeight && Number(heightValue.unit) === unitPoint) {
   724				itemHeight = Math.max(
   725					0,
   726					heightValue.value - item.marginTop - item.marginBottom,
   727				);
   728			} else {
   729				itemHeight = Math.max(
   730					0,
   731					rowSpanSize - item.marginTop - item.marginBottom,
   732				);
   733			}
   734	
   735			itemNode.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE);
   736			itemNode.setPosition(
   737				Yoga.EDGE_TOP,
   738				borderTop + paddingTop + rowStart - item.marginTop,
   739			);
   740			itemNode.setHeight(itemHeight);
   741		}
   742	};
   743	
   744	const isGridContainer = (node: DOMElement): boolean =>
   745		node.style.display === 'grid';
   746	
   747	const restoreGridState = (node: DOMElement): void => {
   748		const state = node.internal_grid;
   749		const {yogaNode} = node;
   750	
   751		if (!state || !yogaNode) {
   752			return;
   753		}
   754	
   755		if (Number(state.width.unit) === unitPoint) {
   756			yogaNode.setWidth(state.width.value);
   757		} else if (Number(state.width.unit) === unitPercent) {
   758			yogaNode.setWidthPercent(state.width.value);
   759		} else {
   760			yogaNode.setWidthAuto();
   761		}
   762	
   763		if (Number(state.height.unit) === unitPoint) {
   764			yogaNode.setHeight(state.height.value);
   765		} else if (Number(state.height.unit) === unitPercent) {
   766			yogaNode.setHeightPercent(state.height.value);
   767		} else {
   768			yogaNode.setHeightAuto();
   769		}
   770	};
   771	
   772	const storeGridState = (node: DOMElement): void => {
   773		const {yogaNode} = node;
   774		if (!yogaNode || node.internal_grid) {
   775			return;
   776		}
   777	
   778		node.internal_grid = {
   779			width: yogaNode.getWidth(),
   780			height: yogaNode.getHeight(),
   781			positionType: yogaNode.getPositionType(),
   782		};
   783	};
   784	
   785	const resetGridLayout = (rootNode: DOMElement): boolean => {
   786		let foundGrid = false;
   787	
   788		const visit = (node: DOMElement): void => {
   789			const isGrid = isGridContainer(node);
   790	
   791			if (node.internal_grid) {
   792				restoreGridState(node);
   793	
   794				if (node.parentNode && !isGridContainer(node.parentNode)) {
   795					node.yogaNode?.setPositionType(node.internal_grid.positionType);
   796					delete node.internal_grid;
   797				}
   798			}
   799	
   800			if (!isGrid) {
   801				delete node.internal_gridLayout;
   802			}
   803	
   804			if (isGrid) {
   805				foundGrid = true;
   806	
   807				if (node.yogaNode) {
   808					storeGridState(node);
   809	
   810					for (const child of node.childNodes) {
   811						if (
   812							child.nodeName === '#text' ||
   813							!child.yogaNode ||
   814							child.yogaNode.getDisplay() === Yoga.DISPLAY_NONE
   815						) {
   816							continue;
   817						}
   818	
   819						storeGridState(child);
   820						child.yogaNode.setPositionType(Yoga.POSITION_TYPE_ABSOLUTE);
   821					}
   822				}
   823			}
   824	
   825			for (const child of node.childNodes) {
   826				if (child.nodeName !== '#text') {
   827					visit(child);
   828				}
   829			}
   830		};
   831	
   832		visit(rootNode);
   833		return foundGrid;
   834	};
   835	
   836	/**
   837	Calculate layout for the whole tree, including CSS grid support.
   838	
   839	Yoga doesn't support grid layout natively, so grid containers are laid out
   840	manually. The layout process runs in three passes:
   841	
   842	1. Grid children are reset to their natural size and measured by Yoga.
   843	2. Column tracks are resolved and children are positioned horizontally.
   844	3. Row tracks are resolved using the heights produced by the second pass and
   845	   children are positioned vertically.
   846	*/
   847	export const calculateLayout = (rootNode: DOMElement): void => {
   848		const hasGrid = resetGridLayout(rootNode);
   849	
   850		rootNode.yogaNode?.calculateLayout(undefined, undefined, Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR);
   851	
   852		if (hasGrid) {
   853			const hasActiveGrid = applyGridColumns(rootNode);
   854	
   855			if (hasActiveGrid) {
   856				rootNode.yogaNode?.calculateLayout(
   857					undefined,
   858					undefined,
   859					Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR,
   860				);
   861	
   862				applyGridRows(rootNode);
   863	
   864				rootNode.yogaNode?.calculateLayout(
   865					undefined,
   866					undefined,
   867					Yoga.DIRECTION_LTR,
   868				);
   869			}
   870		}
   871	};

import React from 'react';
import {Box, Text} from './src/index.js';
import {renderAsync} from './test/helpers/test-renderer.js';

async function main() {
	const instance = await renderAsync(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="10 10" width={20}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 20},
	);
	console.log('fixed:', JSON.stringify(instance.getOutput()));

	await instance.rerenderAsync(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="1fr 1fr" width={20}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
	);
	console.log('fr:', JSON.stringify(instance.getOutput()));

	await instance.rerenderAsync(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="3 3 3" gap={1} width={11}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
		</Box>,
	);
	console.log('three:', JSON.stringify(instance.getOutput()));

	await instance.unmount();
}

main().catch(error => {
	console.error(error);
	process.exit(1);
});
EOF

COMMAND:
cd /app && cat test/grid.tsx
OUTPUT:
import React from 'react';
import test from 'ava';
import {Box, Text} from '../src/index.js';
import {
	renderToString,
	renderToStringAsync,
} from './helpers/render-to-string.js';

test('display grid places children into columns', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="10 10" width={20}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
			<Text>D</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 20},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A         B\nC         D');
});

test('grid gap applies to tracks', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" gap={1} width={11}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 11},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A     B');
});

test('grid column and row gap apply to tracks', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box
			display="grid"
			gridTemplateColumns="3 3"
			columnGap={1}
			rowGap={1}
			width={7}
		>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
			<Text>D</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 7},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A   B\n\nC   D');
});

test('fr columns distribute remaining space', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="1fr 2fr" width={12}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 12},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A   B');
});

test('minmax with fr max distributes remaining space after minimums', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box
			display="grid"
			gridTemplateColumns="minmax(10, 1fr) minmax(20, 1fr)"
			width={40}
		>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 40},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A              B');
});

test('rows are created automatically as needed', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" width={10}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
			<Text>D</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 10},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A    B\nC    D');
});

test('grid without column template uses a single column', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box display="grid" width={10}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 10},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A\nB');
});

test('children can be explicitly placed with gridColumn and gridRow', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box
			display="grid"
			gridTemplateColumns="5 5"
			gridTemplateRows="5 5"
			width={10}
			height={10}
		>
			<Box gridColumn="2" gridRow="2">
				<Text>D</Text>
			</Box>
			<Box>
				<Text>A</Text>
			</Box>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 10},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A\n\n\n\n\n     D\n\n\n\n');
});

test('gridColumn and gridRow accept start / end string', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5 5" width={15}>
			<Box gridColumn="1 / 3">
				<Text>AB</Text>
			</Box>
			<Text>C</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 15},
	);

	t.is(output, 'AB        C');
});

test('Text children can be explicitly placed', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" width={10}>
			<Text gridColumn="2">B</Text>
			<Text gridColumn="1">A</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 10},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A    B');
});

test('grid - concurrent', async t => {
	const output = await renderToStringAsync(
		<Box display="grid" gridTemplateColumns="5 5" width={10}>
			<Text>A</Text>
			<Text>B</Text>
			<Text>C</Text>
		</Box>,
		{columns: 10},
	);

	t.is(output, 'A    B\nC');
});

test('gridTemplateRows with fixed tracks', t => {
	const output = renderToString(
